# Rulings index

One row per ruling. Each ruling lives in its own file, `docs/rulings/<ID>.md`.
Form: `Ruling <id> - <decision>. <evidence>. Cost if wrong: <one sentence>.`

To add a ruling: create `docs/rulings/<ID>.md` and add its row here (one line, same ID).
Never append to `docs/RULINGS.md` (frozen pointer; its entries were split out into this folder).
`tests/unit/rulings-index.test.ts` fails when a file is missing from this table or a row has no file.

Where an Ebda ruling (`R-EBDA-*`) and an inherited ruling disagree, the Ebda ruling wins in this repo.

1424 rulings.
